# 3.1 二层膜及三层膜产生的和频光

> 原作：广濑千秋（沿用项目作者归属；独立底本首页未列署名）  
> 中文翻译：章力
>
> **译校状态（2026-10-20）：** 全51页本轮译校与公式转写完成；B163—B190、B210—B227覆盖102个唯一编号式及无编号中间关系。旧译重复与省略已处理，原文疑点Q096—Q107、Q110—Q112保留并就地说明，未采用原文公式修正。
>
> **底本：** `日语原版/Chapter3/chap3-1.pdf`，51页。标题按底本同时列二层膜及三层膜；现有文件路径暂保留。

> **阅读导引（译者编写）：** 先规定振幅反射/透射系数及源点L系数，依次求二层系各界面与体相的电场乘积、和频极化及p/s出射场；随后推广到三层系的四个界面、三个体相位置，完整列出场乘积和极化，并给出可忽略中间层厚度时的替换关系。 三层体系的完整辐射场作者未进一步求出；本译文保留这一范围限制，不把它计为漏译。

作为文件《薄膜产生的和频过程》的延伸，这里求出膜层数为二时的表达式。（当膜层数达到三层或更多时，会形成维数与层数相同的矩阵级数，因而变得复杂。参照文件《嵌入发光层产生的电场》，可以将这里介绍的方法推广并加以公式化。）

> **译者注（范围说明）：** 上段“矩阵级数”的复杂性表述沿用作者原说法，不据此改写2.6的传播矩阵维数约定。两个文件名按此处原文译出，跨文件公式对应关系待后续逐项核对。

## 1. 系数等

### 反射系数与透射系数

将光电场表示为表面固定坐标系中的分量时，反射系数与透射系数如下。利用这些关系，将相应坐标分量表示为入射光电场偏振分量的形式。

$$

r_{1m, s} = \frac{n_1 \cos\theta_1 - n_m \cos\theta_m}{n_1 \cos\theta_1 + n_m \cos\theta_m}, \quad
t_{1m,s} = \frac{2n_1 \cos\theta_1}{n_1 \cos\theta_1 + n_m \cos\theta_m}

$$

$$

r_{1m, p} = \frac{n_1 \cos\theta_m - n_m \cos\theta_1}{n_1 \cos\theta_m + n_m \cos\theta_1}, \quad
t_{1m, p} = \frac{2n_1 \cos\theta_1}{n_1 \cos\theta_m + n_m \cos\theta_1}

$$

$$

r_{1m,x} = -r_{m1,x} = r_{1m,p},\quad
r_{1m,y} = -r_{m1,y} = r_{1m,s},\quad
r_{1m,z} = -r_{m1,z} = -r_{1m,p}

$$

$$

r_{2m,x} = -r_{m2,x} = r_{2m,p},\quad
r_{2m,y} = -r_{m2,y} = r_{2m,s},\quad
r_{2m,z} = -r_{m2,z} = -r_{2m,p}

$$

$$

t_{1m,x} = (\cos\theta_m/\cos\theta_1)t_{1m,p},\quad
t_{1m,y} = t_{1m,s},\quad
t_{1m,z} = (\sin\theta_m/\sin\theta_1)t_{1m,p}

$$

$$

t_{m1,x} = (\cos\theta_1/\cos\theta_m)t_{m1,p},\quad
t_{m1,y} = t_{m1,s},\quad
t_{m1,z} = (\sin\theta_1/\sin\theta_m)t_{m1,p}

$$

$$

t_{2m,x} = (\cos\theta_m/\cos\theta_2)t_{2m,p},\quad
t_{2m,y} = t_{2m,s},\quad
t_{2m,z} = (\sin\theta_m/\sin\theta_2)t_{2m,p}

$$

$$

t_{m2,x} = (\cos\theta_2/\cos\theta_m)t_{m2,p},\quad
t_{m2,y} = t_{m2,s},\quad
t_{m2,z} = (\sin\theta_2/\sin\theta_m)t_{m2,p}

$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{1m,\alpha}t_{m1,\alpha}
 \\ &=1+r_{1m,\alpha}r_{m1,\alpha}=1-r_{1m,\alpha}^2,\\
t_{2m,\alpha}t_{m2,\alpha}
 &=1+r_{2m,\alpha}r_{m2,\alpha}=1-r_{2m,\alpha}^2,
\qquad (\alpha=x,y,z).
\end{aligned}
$$

### L系数

L系数是将和频极化与其产生的和频光电场振幅 $E_{SF}$ 联系起来的系数。如下式所示，电场按各坐标分量、极化按各偏振分量分别定义。

$$
E_{SF,\alpha}=\sum_{\beta}L_{\alpha\beta}P_{\beta}^{SF}
$$

> **原文疑点（Q096，PDF p2，L系数定义段）：** 原文此处将电场称为“坐标分量”、极化称为“偏振分量”，但随后说明及p3系数采用s/p电场与x/y/z极化的对应。这里保留原文措辞；候选为这两处分量名称互换，尚未采用原文修正。应结合下文的具体分量读取L系数。

根据广义Snell折射公式，所产生的和频光同时包含向上（−）和向下（+）两个方向的光。此外，L系数的表达式随极化所在位置而不同。最初推导时，考虑的是无限薄的薄膜m发生极化，并向介质1和介质2出射光的情况，由此将s偏振、p偏振电场振幅与极化的x、y、z分量联系起来；这里将其推广。同时省略公共因子 $4\pi i\omega_{SF}/c$（以波矢代替折射率时，为 $4\pi i\omega_{SF}^{2}/c^{2}$）。向上（−）光指沿反射光方向（−z方向）传播的光，向下（+）光指沿入射光及透射光方向（+z方向）传播的光。

**L系数的记法：** $L_{i/j,s(p),\alpha}$ 表示极化片m′夹在i层与j层之间时，极化的α分量（α=x,y,z）与其产生的光的s或p偏振分量之间的系数。上标−、+分别区分向上光和向下光。

> **译校说明：** 此处按原页恢复 $E_{SF,\alpha}$ 的下标及 $L_{i/j,s(p),\alpha}$ 的下标结构，旧译把后者的层序、偏振及分量写成上标，属于转写错误。这里的L连接极化源与辐射电场，不是2.6的场基变换矩阵。上述公共因子按作者说明省略，不将其重新乘入本页系数；原式折射率比中省略的SF指标也不擅自补齐。

介质1与叠层膜m之间的极化片m′产生和频光时，L系数如下。

$$
L^{-}_{1/m,p,x} = \cos\theta_{m,SF}/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF})
$$

$$
L^{-}_{1/m,s,y} = 1/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

$$
L^{-}_{1/m,p,z} = (n_m/n_{m'})\sin\theta_{m',SF}/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF}) = (n_m/n_{m'})^2\sin\theta_{m,SF}/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF})
$$

$$
L^{+}_{1/m,p,x} = \cos\theta_{1,SF}/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF})
$$

$$
L^{+}_{1/m,s,y} = 1/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

$$
L^{+}_{1/m,p,z} = -(n_1/n_{m'})\sin\theta_{m',SF}/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF}) = -(n_1/n_{m'})^2\sin\theta_{1,SF}/(n_{1,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{1,SF})
$$

介质2与叠层膜m之间的极化片m″产生和频光时，L系数如下。

$$
L^{-}_{2/m,p,x} = \cos\theta_{2,SF}/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF})
$$

$$
L^{-}_{2/m,s,y} = 1/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

$$
L^{-}_{2/m,p,z} = (n_2/n_{m"})\sin\theta_{m",SF}/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF}) = (n_2/n_{m"})^2\sin\theta_{2,SF}/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF})
$$

$$
L^{+}_{2/m,p,x} = \cos\theta_{m,SF}/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF})
$$

$$
L^{+}_{2/m,s,y} = 1/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

$$
L^{+}_{2/m,p,z} = -(n_m/n_{m"})\sin\theta_{m",SF}/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF}) = -(n_m/n_{m"})^2\sin\theta_{m,SF}/(n_{2,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF} + n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{2,SF})
$$

叠层膜内部的极化片产生和频光时，L系数如下。

$$
L^{-}_{m/m,p,x} = L^{+}_{m/m,p,x} = \cos\theta_{m,SF}/(2n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

$$
L^{-}_{m/m,s,y} = L^{+}_{m/m,s,y} = 1/(2n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

$$
L^{-}_{m/m,p,z} = -L^{+}_{m/m,p,z} = \sin\theta_{m,SF}/(2n_{m,SF}\cos\theta_{m,SF})
$$

上述电场与极化之间的关系不限于和频极化及和频光。对于振荡极化及其产生的电磁波，这些关系一般成立。

## 2. 二层系（1/m′/m/2）产生的和频光

### 2.1 电场振幅的乘积

下面给出可见光电场与红外光电场的乘积。其中，相位部分按和频光路径在最外表面反射的情况给出；若还需要计入m′/m界面及m/m″界面处的反射，则必须另行考虑。关于公式的推导，请参阅文件《二层膜产生的和频光（SFG）》第4节或《叠层膜产生的和频过程（SFG）》第4节。

> **原文疑点（Q097，PDF p3，2.1引言）：** 本段位于二层系1/m′/m/2中，却同时提及三层记法中的m/m″界面。原文还引用《二层膜产生的和频光（SFG）》第4节及《叠层膜产生的和频过程（SFG）》第4节；这些文件名与本文或本地文件的对应尚未确认，不据此重定向到某个本地节号。此处保留界面名称、引用文件名和节号。m/m″的提及是否为兼顾后文三层系，留待后续核验。

**1/m′界面的1侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）以及 $E^-$ 源（$n=0$ 时）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(0^-)E_{ir,\beta}(0^-)
 \\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha})[\,(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta})[\,(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times\exp\!\Bigl[2ni
 (h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m'}\tan\theta_{m',SF})\\
 &\hspace{7em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}
 -k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.1}
$$

> **译校说明：** 式(2.1)从PDF p3接至p4，合为一条可编辑公式；分子、分母及指数仅分行，所有因子与原层标保留。原文此处仅在源的分类中标出n=0，尚未单独定义n；不把它解释为折射率或自行补定其计数含义。此处保留的 $k_{m,ir}$、$k_{m,vis}$ 和相应角标未改写为其他层指标。“最外表面”原文为“最外面”，旧译“最小侧面”已据原文纠正，属于翻译错误修复。

**1/m′界面的m′侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(0^+)E_{ir,\beta}(0^+)
 \\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[\,(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&t_{1m',ir,\beta}[\,(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times\exp\!\Bigl[2ni
 (h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m'}\tan\theta_{m',SF})\\
 &\hspace{7em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}
 -k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.2}
$$

**m/2界面的2侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+h_m^+)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+h_m^+)
 \\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_m)}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_m)}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times\exp\!\Bigl[2ni
 (h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m'}\tan\theta_{m',SF})\\
 &\hspace{7em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}
 -k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.3}
$$

（b）：$E^-$ 源（$n>0$ 时，经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+h_m^+)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+h_m^+)
 \\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_m)}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_m)}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times\exp\!\Bigl[i(2n+1)
 (h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m'}\tan\theta_{m',SF})\\
 &\hspace{7em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}
 -k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.4}
$$

**m/2界面的m侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源（经反射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+h_m^-)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+h_m^-)
 \\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(1+r_{m2,vis,\alpha})e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_m)}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}(1+r_{m2,ir,\beta})e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_m)}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times\exp\!\Bigl[i(2n+1)
 (h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m'}\tan\theta_{m',SF})\\
 &\hspace{7em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}
 -k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.5}
$$

**m′/m界面的m′侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}^{-})E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}^{-}) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[6pt]&\quad \times

\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]&\quad \times

\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+ (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]&\quad \times \exp\!\Bigl[i h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF}\\ &\hspace{6em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]\\[6pt]&\quad \times \exp\!\Bigl[i(2n + 1)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})\\ &\hspace{6em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.6}
$$

（b）：$E^+$ 源（经反射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}^{-})E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}^{-}) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[6pt]&\quad \times

\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]&\quad \times

\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+ (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]&\quad \times \exp\!\Bigl[i h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF}\\ &\hspace{6em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr]\\[6pt]&\quad \times \exp\!\Bigl[2ni(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})\\ &\hspace{6em}\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})\Bigr] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.7}
$$

**m′/m界面的m侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}^{+})E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}^{+}) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1 + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[i h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF}(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[2ni(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.8}
$$

（b）：$E^+$ 源（经反射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}^{+})E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}^{+}) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1 + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[i h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF}(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[2ni(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir} - k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.9}
$$


> **原文疑点（Q098，PDF p5—6，式2.8—2.9）：** 式2.8的红外光分子沿用 $(1+r_{m\prime m,ir,\beta})$，而可见光分子及式2.9使用对应的透射系数；两式末端相位又同为2ni。这里逐处保留，不把红外因子或相位静默统一。候选为将2.8红外因子改为对应透射系数；两式源分类与路径相位是否对应仍待核验。

**m′层中深度 $z_1$ 处：**

$[A_n^{(m')}]$：$E^+$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(z_1) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}) \exp[iz_1 k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}} \exp[iz_1 k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i(2n + 2)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.10a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{ir,\beta}(z_1) \\ &=E^{0}_{ir,\beta} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}) \exp[iz_1 k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} \exp[iz_1 k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i(2n + 2)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.10b}
$$

两者的乘积为：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(z_1)E_{ir,\beta}(z_1) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1 + r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i(\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i(2n + 2)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})(k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir})] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.11}
$$


> **原文疑点（Q099，PDF p6—7，式2.10b、2.12b）：** 这两个红外场单独表达式的末端相位仍写 $k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}$，但乘积式2.11、2.13的总相位含vis与ir两项之和。保留原值；候选为单独红外场末端采用ir指标，尚未采用。

$[B_n^{(m')}]$：$E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(z_1) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}) \exp[iz_1 k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}} \exp[iz_1 k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i2n(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.12a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{ir,\beta}(z_1) \\ &=E^{0}_{ir,\beta} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}) \exp[iz_1 k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} \exp[iz_1 k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i2n(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.12b}
$$

两者的乘积为：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(z_1)E_{ir,\beta}(z_1) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1 + r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i(\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i2n(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})(k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir})] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.13}
$$

**m层中距m′/m界面深度 $z_2$ 处：**

$[A_n^{(m)}]$：$E^+$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m + z_2) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2 k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}} \exp[iz_2 k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i(2n + 2)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.14a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{ir,\beta}(h_m + z_2) \\ &=E^{0}_{ir,\beta} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2 k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}} \exp[iz_2 k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i(2n + 2)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.14b}
$$

两者的乘积为：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m + z_2)E_{ir,\beta}(h_m + z_2) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i(\beta_{m,ir}+\beta_{m,vis})h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i(2n + 2)(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})(k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir})] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.15}
$$


> **原文疑点（Q100，PDF p7—9，m层体相段）：** 原文标题将 $z_2$ 定为距m′/m界面的深度，但场位置写 $h_m+z_2$；与前文层序比较，候选为 $h_{m\prime}+z_2$，此处不改。p8的反向源标题又写 $B_n^{(m\prime)}$，旧译改成了m；现恢复原层标，候选为m，尚未采用。


> **原文疑点（Q101，PDF p8—9，式2.15、2.17）：** 四项展开式在每项指数前重复出现左花括号，末尾未见与之配对的右花括号。这里保留可见括号而不赋予候选分组。候选为仅保留一对包住四项和的花括号；尚未采用原文修正。

$[B_n^{(m')}]$：$E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m + z_2) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2 k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}} \exp[iz_2 k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i2n(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.16a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{ir,\beta}(h_m + z_2) \\ &=E^{0}_{ir,\beta} \\ &\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2 k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}} \exp[iz_2 k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i2n(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.16b}
$$

两者的乘积为：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m + z_2)E_{ir,\beta}(h_m + z_2) \\ &=E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i(\beta_{m,ir}+\beta_{m,vis})h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[-i2n(h_m \tan\theta_{m,SF} + h_{m'} \tan\theta_{m',SF})(k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis}\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir})] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.17}
$$

### 2.2 和频极化

各位置的和频极化，是该处可见光与红外光的电场乘积乘以极化率得到的。下面列出去除相位部分后的极化振幅。

> **译者注（记号与范围）：** 此处χ表示二阶非线性极化率，正文在阶数明确的上下文中简称“极化率”；原式未显式写出的阶数上标不另行补入。保留原文的 $P_a^*$ 记号；本段未单独解释星号，不据一般习惯将其改写成复共轭或赋予新的定义。“去除相位部分”沿用作者的限定；以下仍保留分式内的膜内传播指数，不据此把全部指数项删去。式(2.18)的求和作用于含α、β的完整场乘积，两条频率分式随同前因子在求和内读取。

**1/m′界面的1侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）以及 $E^-$ 源（$n=0$ 时）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(0^-)
 \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha})[\,(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta})[\,(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.18}
$$

**1/m′界面的m′侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(0^+)
 \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[\,(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_m}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]
 &\quad\times \frac{\begin{aligned}&t_{1m',ir,\beta}[\,(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})\\&\qquad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\,]\end{aligned}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_m}\end{aligned}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.19}
$$

**m/2界面的2侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m'} + h_{m}^{+}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[6pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.20}
$$

（b）：$E^-$ 源（$n>0$ 时，经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m'} + h_{m}^{+}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[6pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}\\[6pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{\begin{aligned}&1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.21}
$$

**m/2 界面的 m 侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源（经反射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m'} + h_{m}^{-}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(1 + r_{m2,vis,\alpha})e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}(1 + r_{m2,ir,\beta})e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.22}
$$

**m'/m 界面的 m' 侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m}^{-}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.23}
$$

（b）：$E^+$ 源（经反射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m}^{-}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.24}
$$



> **原文疑点（Q102，PDF p10—11，式2.23—2.26）：** 内部界面极化的位置写 $h_m^-$、$h_m^+$，但2.1对应场写 $h_{m\prime}^{\mp}$。各处按原文保留，候选为极化式厚度下标补撇号，未采用。

**m'/m 界面的 m 侧：**

（a）：$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m}^{+}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1 + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.25}
$$

（b）：$E^+$ 源（经反射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(h_{m}^{+}) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1 + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.26}
$$



> **原文疑点（Q103，PDF p11，式2.25）：** 红外光分子仍采用 $(1+r_{m\prime m,ir,\beta})$，与同式可见光分子及2.26的对应透射系数不同，沿原式保留。候选为改用对应透射系数，关联Q098，未采用。

**m' 层的深度 $z_1$ 点：**

$[A_n^{(m')}]$：$E^+$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(z_1) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1 + r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1+ \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i(\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\} 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.27}
$$

$[B_n^{(m')}]$：$E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(z_1) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1 + r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i(\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir}))]\} 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.28}
$$

**m 层中距 m'/m 界面深度 $z_2$ 的点：**

$[A_n^{(m)}]$：$E^+$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(z_2) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1+ \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i(\beta_{m,ir}+\beta_{m,vis})h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1 + \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.29}
$$

$[B_n^{(m)}]$：$E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^{*}(z_2) \\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta} \chi_{a\alpha\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\\&\quad\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[4pt]&\quad \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))]\\[4pt]&\quad +r_{m2,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i(\beta_{m,ir}+\beta_{m,vis})h_{m}} \times\{\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1 - \tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})\\ &\hspace{5em}+ k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir}))] 
\end{aligned}
\tag{2.30}
$$



> **原文疑点（Q104，PDF p12—13，式2.29—2.30）：** 四项和中重复出现左花括号、未配右花括号，保留可见符号，关联Q101。候选为只用一对花括号包住四项和，未采用。此处原极化位置只写z2，不静默补成2.1的hm+z2。

### 2.3 和频电场

利用另一文件「双层膜的和频(和频)——一般式」中的结果，可以导出以下表达式。此外，关于相位部分仅记其结果。

**1/m' 界面 1 侧极化所产生的 和频：**

(a): 反射方向

零次光产生的 $E^{-}(0^{-}) + r_{1m'}E^{+}(0^{-})$ 之外，还需加上进入内部的光经多重反射后出射的分量。也就是说，将文件「双层膜的和频(和频)——一般式」（以下简称文件「一般式」）的 (2.1) 式中的 $L^{+}_{m'/m'}P(z_1)$ 置换为 $t_{1m'}L^{+}_{1/1}P(z_1 = 0)$ 后所得的分量。由 $a_0^{*} = a$、$a_0 = 1$，得到下式：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \{L^{-}_{1/1} + L^{+}_{1/1}[r_{1m'} + \frac{t_{1m'}t_{m'1}(r_{m'm} + r_{m2}b^{2})a^{2}}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}}]\}P^{*}(z_1 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&= \frac{(L^{-}_{1/1} + r_{1m'}L^{+}_{1/1})(1 + r_{m'm}r_{m2}b^{2}) + (r_{1m'}L^{-}_{1/1} + L^{+}_{1/1})(r_{m'm} + r_{m2}b^{2})a^{2}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&(L^{-}_{1/1,px} + r_{1m',p}L^{+}_{1/1,px}) \\ &= (r_{1m',p}L^{-}_{1/1,px} + L^{+}_{1/1,px}) = L^{-}_{1/m',px}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&(L^{-}_{1/1,sy} + r_{1m',s}L^{+}_{1/1,sy}) \\ &= (r_{1m',s}L^{-}_{1/1,sy} + L^{+}_{1/1,sy}) = L^{-}_{1/m',sy}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&(L^{-}_{1/1,pz} + r_{1m',p}L^{+}_{1/1,pz}) \\ &= -(r_{1m',p}L^{-}_{1/1,pz} + L^{+}_{1/1,pz}) = L^{-}_{1/m',pz} \quad (n_{m"} = n_1)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{(1+ r_{m'm}r_{m2}b^{2}) \pm (r_{m'm} + r_{m2}b^{2})a^{2}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} L^{-}_{1/m'}P^{*}(z_1 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

(式中上符号对应 x 和 y 分量，下符号对应 z 分量)

代入 (2.18) 式，得：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net},p} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha})[(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta})[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times[

\frac{(1 + r_{m'm,SF,p}r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} L^{-}_{1/m',px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}


\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&+

\frac{(1 + r_{m'm,SF,p}r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}) - (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}}{1 + r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} L^{-}_{1/m',pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}] \quad (n_{m"} \\ &= n_1) 
\end{aligned}\tag{2.31a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net},s} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha})[(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta})[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1 + r_{m'm,SF,s}r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} L^{-}_{1/m',sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta} 
\end{aligned}\tag{2.31b}
$$

(b): 透射方向

仅有 $E^{+}(0^{-})$ 进入膜内并穿透至对面。与上文同理，将文件「一般式」的 (2.2) 式中的 $L^{+}_{m'/m'}P(z_1)$ 置换为 $t_{1m'}L^{+}_{1/1}P(z_1 = 0)$，并令 $a_0^{*} = a$、$a_0 = 1$，得到下式：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{1m'}t_{m2}t_{m'm}ab}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} L^{+}_{1/1}P^{*}(z_1 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{1m',p}L^{+}_{1/1,px} \\ &= L^{+}_{1/m',px}, \quad
t_{1m',s}L^{+}_{1/1,sy} = L^{+}_{1/m',sy}, \quad
t_{1m',p}L^{+}_{1/1,pz} = L^{+}_{1/m',pz} \quad (n_{m"} = n_1)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{m'm}t_{m2}ab}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} L^{+}_{1/m'}P^{*}(z_1 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

代入 (2.18) 式，得：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net},p} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha})[(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta})[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{t_{1m',SF,p}t_{m2,SF,p}e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}+\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'})}(L^{+}_{1/m',px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta} + L^{+}_{1/m',pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta})}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} \quad (n_{m"}=n_1) 
\end{aligned}\tag{2.32a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net},s} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha})[(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta})[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{t_{1m',SF,s}t_{m2,SF,s}e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}+\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'})}L^{+}_{1/m',sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} 
\end{aligned}\tag{2.32b}
$$

---

**1/m′界面m′侧的极化产生的和频光：**

（a）反射方向。

在文件《一般式》的(2.1)和(2.3)式中，令 $z_1=0$、$a_0^*=a$、$a_0=1$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)_{net}\\ &=\frac{t_{m\prime1}[(r_{m\prime m}+r_{m2}b^2)a^2L^+_{m\prime/m\prime}+(1+r_{m\prime m}r_{m2}b^2)L^-_{m\prime/m\prime}]}{1+r_{1m\prime}r_{m\prime m}a^2+(r_{m\prime m}+r_{1m\prime}a^2)r_{m2}b^2}P^*(z_1=0)
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，

$$
\begin{aligned}&t_{1m\prime,p}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime,px}\\ &=t_{1m\prime,p}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime,px}=L^-_{1/m\prime,px},\\t_{1m\prime,s}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime,sy}&=t_{1m\prime,s}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime,sy}=L^-_{1/m\prime,sy},\\t_{1m\prime,p}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime,pz}&=-t_{1m\prime,p}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime,pz}=L^-_{1/m\prime,pz}\quad(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m\prime}).\end{aligned}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)_{net}\\ &=\frac{t_{m\prime1}[(1+r_{m\prime m}r_{m2}b^2)\pm(r_{m\prime m}+r_{m2}b^2)a^2]}{1+r_{1m\prime}r_{m\prime m}a^2+(r_{m\prime m}+r_{1m\prime}a^2)r_{m2}b^2}L^-_{1/m}P^*(z_1=0)
\end{aligned}
$$

上符号用于x、y分量，下符号用于z分量。由式(2.19)，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net},p} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}




\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}




\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}




\\[4pt]&\times[

\frac{(1 + r_{m'm,SF,p}r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} L^{-}_{1/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}






\\&\quad+ \frac{(1 + r_{m'm,SF,p}r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}) - (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}}{1 + r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} L^{-}_{1/m,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}] \quad (n_{m''} = n_{m'}) 
\end{aligned}\tag{2.33a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net},s} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}




\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[(1 + r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}




\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}




\\[4pt]&\times

\frac{(1 + r_{m'm,SF,s}r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} L^{-}_{1/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta} 
\end{aligned}\tag{2.33b}
$$

（b）透射方向。

在文件《一般式》的(2.2)和(2.4)式中，令 $z_1=0$、$a_0^*=a$、$a_0=1$，得到

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)_{net}\\ &=\frac{t_{m2}ab[t_{m\prime m}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime}+r_{m\prime1}t_{m\prime m}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime}]}{1+r_{1m\prime}r_{m\prime m}a^2+(r_{m\prime m}+r_{1m\prime}a^2)r_{m2}b^2}P^*(z_1=0)
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，

$$
\begin{aligned}&t_{m\prime m,p}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime,px}\\ &=t_{m\prime m,p}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime,px}=L^+_{m\prime/m,px},\\t_{m\prime m,s}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime,sy}&=t_{m\prime m,s}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime,sy}=L^+_{m\prime/m,sy},\\t_{m\prime m,p}L^+_{m\prime/m\prime,pz}&=-t_{m\prime m,p}L^-_{m\prime/m\prime,pz}=L^+_{m\prime/m,pz}\quad(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m\prime}).\end{aligned}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)_{net}\\ &=\frac{t_{m2}ab(1\pm r_{m\prime1})}{1+r_{1m\prime}r_{m\prime m}a^2+(r_{m\prime m}+r_{1m\prime}a^2)r_{m2}b^2}L^-_{m\prime/m}P^*(z_1=0)
\end{aligned}
$$

上符号用于x、y分量，下符号用于z分量。由式(2.19)，



$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net},p} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times
\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times
\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times
\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}+\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'})}[(1+ r_{m'1,SF,p})L^{+}_{m'/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta} + (1- r_{m'1,SF,p})L^{+}_{m'/m,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}]}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}} \quad (n_{m''} = n_{m'})

\end{aligned}\tag{2.34a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net},s} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times
\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times
\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}[(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}]}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times
\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}+\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'})}(1+ r_{m'1,SF,s})L^{+}_{m'/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.34b}
$$

---

**m/2 界面的 2 侧极化产生的和频：**

（a）反射方向

仅有 $E^{-}(h_{m}^{+})$ 进入薄膜内部并穿透到另一侧。在文件「一般式」的 (2.7) 式中，令 $z_2 = h_m$、$b_0^{*} = 1$、$b_0 = b$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{2m}t_{m'1}b^{2}(r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})L^{-}_{2/2}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{+})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{2m,p}L^{-}_{2/2,px} \\ &= L^{-}_{m/2,px},\quad t_{2m,s}L^{-}_{2/2,sy} = L^{-}_{m/2,sy},\quad t_{2m,p}L^{-}_{2/2,pz} = L^{-}_{m/2,pz} \;(n_{m''} = n_2)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{m'1}b^{2}(r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})L^{-}_{m/2}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{+})
\end{aligned}
$$

参照 (2.20) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,p}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{2m,SF,p}t_{m'1,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}(r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})[L^{-}_{m/2,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta} + L^{-}_{m/2,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}]}{1 + r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.35a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,s}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{2m,SF,s}t_{m'1,SF,s}(r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}} L^{-}_{m/2,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.35b}
$$

（b）透射方向

除了零次光产生的 $E^{+}(z_2 = h_m^{+}) + r_{2m}E^{-}(z_2 = h_m^{+})$ 之外，还需加上进入内部的光经多次反射后出射的贡献，即在文件「一般式」的 (2.8) 式中，将 $L^{-}_{m/m}P(z_2)$ 替换为 $t_{1m'}L^{-}_{2/2}P(z_2 = h_m^{+})$ 后所得的部分。此外，令 $z_2 = h_m$、$b_0^{*} = 1$、$b_0 = b$。
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \{L^{+}_{2/2} + L^{-}_{2/2}[r_{2m} - \frac{t_{2m}t_{m2}b^{2}(r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}}]\}P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{+})
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&= \frac{(1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2})(L^{+}_{2/2} + r_{2m}L^{-}_{2/2}) + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})b^{2}(r_{m2}L^{+}_{2/2} + L^{-}_{2/2})}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{+})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&(L^{+}_{2/2,px} + r_{2m,p}L^{-}_{2/2,px}) \\ &= (r_{m2,p}L^{+}_{2/2,px} + L^{-}_{2/2,px}) = L^{+}_{2/m,px}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&(L^{+}_{2/2,sy} + r_{2m,s}L^{-}_{2/2,sy}) \\ &= (r_{m2,s}L^{+}_{2/2,sy} + L^{-}_{2/2,sy}) = L^{+}_{2/m,sy}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&(L^{+}_{2/2,pz} + r_{2m,p}L^{-}_{2/2,pz}) \\ &= -(r_{m2,p}L^{+}_{2/2,pz} + L^{-}_{2/2,pz}) = L^{+}_{2/m,pz} \;(n_{m''} = n_2)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{(1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2}) \pm (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})b^{2}}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} L^{+}_{2/m}P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{+})
\end{aligned}
$$

（x 和 y 分量取上符号，z 分量取下符号）

参照 (2.20) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,p}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times\Bigg\{
\frac{[(1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}]L^{-}_{2/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&+\frac{[(1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}) - (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}]L^{-}_{2/m,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\Bigg\}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.36a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,s}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{[(1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}]L^{-}_{2/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.36b}
$$
---

**m/2 界面的 m 侧极化产生的和频：**

（a）反射方向

在文件「一般式」的 (2.5) 式和 (2.7) 式中，令 $z_2 = h_m$、$b_0^{*} = 1$、$b_0 = b$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{ab\,t_{mm'}(r_{m2}L^{+}_{m/m} + L^{-}_{m/m})}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{-})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{mm',p}L^{+}_{m/m,px} \\ &= t_{mm',p}L^{-}_{m/m,px} = L^{-}_{m'/m,px},\quad t_{mm',s}L^{+}_{m/m,sy} = t_{mm',s}L^{-}_{m/m,sy} = L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{mm',p}L^{+}_{m/m,pz} \\ &= t_{mm',p}L^{-}_{m/m,pz} = -L^{-}_{m'/m,px} \;(n_{m''} = n_m)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{ab(1 \pm r_{m2})L^{-}_{m'/m}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{-})
\end{aligned}
$$

（x 和 y 分量取上符号，z 分量取下符号）

参照 (2.23) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,p}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,SF}h_{m})}[(1+ r_{m2,SF,p})L^{-}_{m'/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta} + (1- r_{m2,SF,p})L^{-}_{m'/m,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}]}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.37a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,s}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,SF}h_{m})}(1+ r_{m2,SF,s})L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.37b}
$$
---

**m/2 界面的 m 侧（透射方向）**

在文件「一般式」的 (2.6) 式和 (2.8) 式中，令 $z_2 = h_m$、$b_0^{*} = 1$、$b_0 = b$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{(1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2})L^{+}_{m/m} - (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})b^{2}L^{-}_{m/m}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{-})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m2,p}L^{+}_{m/m,px} \\ &= t_{m2,p}L^{-}_{m/m,px} = L^{+}_{m/2,px},\quad t_{m2,s}L^{+}_{m/m,sy} = t_{m2,s}L^{-}_{m/m,sy} = L^{+}_{m/2,sy}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m2,p}L^{+}_{m/m,pz} \\ &= -t_{m2,p}L^{-}_{m/m,pz} = L^{+}_{m/2,pz} \;(n_{m''} = n_m)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{[(1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2}) \mp (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})b^{2}]L^{+}_{m/2}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = h_{m}^{-})
\end{aligned}
$$

（x,y取上符号，z取下符号）

参照 (2.23) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,p}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times\Bigg\{
\frac{[(1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}) - (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}]L^{+}_{m/2,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&+
\frac{[(1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}) + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}]L^{+}_{m/2,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\Bigg\}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.38a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,s}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{[(1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}) - (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}]L^{+}_{m/2,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.38b}
$$
---

**m'/m 界面的 m' 侧极化产生的和频：**

（a）反射方向

在文件「一般式」的 (2.1) 式和 (2.3) 式中，令 $z_1 = h_{m'}$、$a_0^{*} = 1$、$a_0 = a$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{a\,t_{m'1}[(r_{m'm} + r_{m2}b^{2})L^{+}_{m'/m'} + (1+ r_{mm'}r_{m2}b^{2})L^{-}_{m'/m'}]}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m'1,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',px} \\ &= t_{m'1,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',px} = L^{-}_{1/m',px},\quad t_{m'1,s}L^{+}_{m'/m',sy} = t_{m'1,s}L^{-}_{m'/m',sy} = L^{-}_{1/m',sy}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m'1,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',pz} \\ &= t_{m'1,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',pz} = -L^{-}_{1/m',px} \;(n_{m''} = n_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{a[(1 + r_{mm'}r_{m2}b^{2}) \pm (r_{m'm} + r_{m2}b^{2})]L^{-}_{1/m'}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&= \frac{a(1 \pm r_{mm'})(1 \pm r_{m2}b^{2})L^{-}_{1/m'}}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

（x 和 y 分量取上符号，z 分量取下符号）

参照 (2.23) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,p}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
