&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times\Bigg\{
\frac{e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}[(1+ r_{mm',SF,p})(1+ r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})]L^{-}_{1/m',px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&+
\frac{e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}[(1- r_{mm',SF,p})(1- r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})]L^{-}_{1/m',pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\Bigg\}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.39a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,s}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{mm',SF,s})(1+ r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})L^{-}_{1/m',sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.39b}
$$


（b）透射方向

在文件「一般式」的 (2.2) 式和 (2.4) 式中，令 $z_1 = h_{m'}$、$a_0^{*} = 1$、$a_0 = a$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{m2}t_{m'm}b(L^{+}_{m'/m'} + r_{m'1}a^{2}L^{-}_{m'/m'})}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m'm,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',px} \\ &= L^{+}_{m'/m,px},\; t_{m'm,s}L^{+}_{m'/m',sy} = L^{+}_{m'/m,sy},\; t_{m'm,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',pz} = -t_{m'm,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',pz} = L^{+}_{m'/m,pz} \;(n_{m''} = n_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{m2}b(1 \pm r_{m'1}a^{2})L^{+}_{m'/m}}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

（x,y取上符号，z取下符号）

参照 (2.23) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,p}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{m2,SF,p}e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}[(1+ r_{m'1,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m'/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta} + (1- r_{m'1,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m'/m,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}]}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.40a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,s}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'm,ir,\beta})(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{m2,SF,s}e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m'1,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m'/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.40b}
$$


---

**m'/m 界面的 m 侧极化产生的和频：**

（a）反射方向

在文件「一般式」的 (2.5) 式和 (2.7) 式中，令 $z_2 = 0$、$b_0^{*} = b$、$b_0 = 1$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{a\,t_{m'1}t_{m'm}(r_{m2}b^{2}L^{+}_{m'/m'} + L^{-}_{m'/m'})}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{mm',p}L^{+}_{m/m,px} \\ &= t_{mm',p}L^{-}_{m/m,px} = L^{-}_{m'/m,px},\; t_{mm',s}L^{+}_{m/m,sy} = t_{mm',s}L^{-}_{m/m,sy} = L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{mm',p}L^{+}_{m/m,pz} \\ &= -t_{mm',p}L^{-}_{m/m,pz} = -L^{-}_{m'/m,pz} \;(n_{m''} = n_m)
\end{aligned}
$$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{a\,t_{m'1}(1 \pm r_{m2}b^{2})L^{-}_{m'/m}}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_1 = h_{m'})
\end{aligned}
$$

（x,y取上符号，z取下符号）

参照 (2.25) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,p}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{m'1,SF,p}e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}[(1+ r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})L^{-}_{m'/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta} + (1- r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})L^{-}_{m'/m,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}]}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.41a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{-}_{1,s}(0^{-})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{m'1,SF,s}e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.41b}
$$

（b）透射方向

在文件「一般式」的 (2.6) 式和 (2.8) 式中，令 $z_2 = 0$、$b_0^{*} = b$、$b_0 = 1$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{t_{m2}b[(1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2})L^{+}_{m/m} - (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})L^{-}_{m/m}]}{1 + r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

其中，$t_{m2,p}L^{+}_{m/m,px} = t_{m2,p}L^{-}_{m/m,px} = L^{+}_{m/2,px}$，$t_{m2,s}L^{+}_{m/m,sy} = t_{m2,s}L^{-}_{m/m,sy} = L^{+}_{m/2,sy}$，$t_{m2,p}L^{+}_{m/m,pz} = -t_{m2,p}L^{-}_{m/m,pz} = L^{+}_{m/2,pz}$ $(n_{m''} = n_m)$

因此，
$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \frac{b(1 \mp r_{m'm})(1 \mp r_{1m'}a^{2})L^{+}_{m/2}}{1+ r_{1m'}r_{m'm}a^{2} + (r_{m'm} + r_{1m'}a^{2})r_{m2}b^{2}} P^{*}(z_2 = 0)
\end{aligned}
$$

（x,y取上符号，z取下符号）

参照 (2.25) 式，

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,p}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times\Bigg\{
\frac{e^{i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}(1- r_{m'm,SF,p})(1- r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m/2,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&+\frac{e^{i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}(1+ r_{m'm,SF,p})(1+ r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m/2,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,p} + r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}
\Bigg\}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.42a}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E^{+}_{2,s}(h_{m'}+h_{m}^{+})_{\mathrm{net}} \\ &= \sum_{\alpha,\beta} E^{0}_{vis,\alpha}E^{0}_{ir,\beta}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+ r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+ (r_{m'm,vis,\alpha} + r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+ r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})}{1+ r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,ir,\beta} + r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\\[4pt]&\times \frac{e^{i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}(1- r_{m'm,SF,s})(1- r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m/2,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}}{1+ r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}} + (r_{m'm,SF,s} + r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}}

\end{aligned}\tag{2.42b}
$$

---



> **原文疑点（Q105，PDF p13—25，界面出射场）：** 原式中的双撇层折射率条件、L的正负号及层名、场位置和引用号均逐处保留，不跨式统一。p19—21的m/2界面m侧引用2.23而非邻近2.22，候选为2.22；2.33与2.34的L层名/正负也有差异，候选须结合源片定义核验。p24—25无编号式中m/2连接因子的m记号、2.42a/b的正反射系数因子亦按原式保留；未采用修正。旧译r的层标“1m,,SF”恢复为原页1m′,SF，属转写修复。

**m′层内极化产生的和频光：**

在文件《一般式》的(2.1)—(2.4)式中，使用下列定义：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&a_0\\ &=e^{ik_{m\prime}z_1/\cos\theta_{m\prime}},\qquad a_0^*=e^{i\beta_{m\prime}h_{m\prime}/\cos\theta_{m\prime}}e^{-ik_{m\prime}z_1/\cos\theta_{m\prime}}
\end{aligned}
$$

不过，本文由(2.10)—(2.17)式导出(2.27)—(2.30)式时所用的光路，没有考虑某一层内部的多重反射；因而相位部分的处理有欠缺。作者指出，可将指数自变量中h/cosθ及z/cosθ形式的部分分别替换为β×h和β×z来修正。下面列出这样替换后的结果。为简便，取

$$
\begin{aligned}
&a_0\\ &=e^{i\beta_{m\prime}z_1},\qquad a_0^*=e^{i\beta_{m\prime}h_{m\prime}}e^{-i\beta_{m\prime}z_1}
\end{aligned}
$$

并和上面同样定义

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

（a）反射方向。

$E^+$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.1)式和本文(2.27)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m'1}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm}+r_{m2}e^{2i\beta_{m}h_{m}})L^{+}_{m'/m'}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

$E^-$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.3)式和本文(2.28)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m'1}(1+r_{mm'}r_{m2}e^{2i\beta_{m}h_{m}})L^{-}_{m'/m'}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

对于由E⁺源产生的和频光，将z_1从0积分至该层厚度；对于由E⁻源产生的和频光，从该层厚度积分至0。再利用下列关系整理：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m'1,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',px}\\ &=L^{-}_{1/m',px}\\[5pt]t_{m'1,s}L^{+}_{m'/m',sy}&=L^{-}_{1/m',sy}\\[5pt]t_{m'1,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',pz}&=-L^{-}_{1/m',pz}\\[5pt]t_{m'1,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',px}&=L^{-}_{1/m',px}\\[5pt]t_{m'1,s}L^{-}_{m'/m',sy}&=L^{-}_{1/m',sy}\\[5pt]t_{m'1,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',pz}&=L^{-}_{1/m',pz}
\end{aligned}
$$

$(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m'})$。

$$
\begin{aligned}

&=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}L^-_{1/m\prime}P_a^*\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{mm',SF,p}r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\\&\qquad\pm(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad （b）透射方向。

$E^+$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.2)式和本文(2.27)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m2}e^{i\beta_{m}h_m}t_{m'm}L^{+}_{m'/m'}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

$E^-$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.4)式和本文(2.28)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m2}e^{i\beta_{m}h_m}t_{m'm}r_{m'1}L^{-}_{m'/m'}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\exp[iz_1(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

对于由E⁺源产生的和频光，将z_1从0积分至该层厚度；对于由E⁻源产生的和频光，从该层厚度积分至0。再利用下列关系整理：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m'm,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',px}\\ &=L^{+}_{m'/m,px}\\[5pt]t_{m'm,s}L^{+}_{m'/m',sy}&=L^{+}_{m'/m,sy}\\[5pt]t_{m'm,p}L^{+}_{m'/m',pz}&=L^{+}_{m'/m,pz}\\[5pt]t_{m'm,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',px}&=L^{+}_{m'/m,px}\\[5pt]t_{m'm,s}L^{-}_{m'/m',sy}&=L^{+}_{m'/m,sy}\\[5pt]t_{m'm,p}L^{-}_{m'/m',pz}&=-L^{+}_{m'/m,pz}
\end{aligned}
$$

$(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m'})$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m2}e^{i(\beta_{m\prime}h_{m\prime}+\beta_mh_m)}L^+_{m\prime/m}P_a^*\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\pm r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\pm r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\pm r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\pm r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

上符号用于x、y分量，下符号用于z分量。因此，

$$
\begin{aligned}

&=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}t_{m2,SF,p}e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,SF}h_m)}\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad &\qquad+r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\\ &\qquad+r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\}L^{+}_{m'/m,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\\ &\qquad-r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\\ &\qquad-r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}-\beta_{m',ir}}\\ &\qquad-r_{m'1,SF,p}\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}-1}{\beta_{m',SF}-\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad $$

&=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}t_{m2,SF,s}e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,SF}h_m)}\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}})(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}})\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad > **原文疑点（Q106，PDF 25—32，体相积分）：** 作者在此明确指出前面相位处理的欠缺，并说明替换方式；这属于原文推导说明，未反向修改2.1/2.2的式子。原定义P星号内含求和，而随后又写求和，按原样保留；δ记号、端点方向和各积分分母符号不擅自消去。m体相的最终式中全局因子、层标与通式有差异，按原页保留，未作为已确认理论修正。

**m层内极化产生的和频光：**

在文件《一般式》的(2.5)—(2.8)式中，使用下列定义：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&b_0\\ &=e^{ik_mz_2/\cos\theta_m},\qquad b_0^*=e^{i\beta_mh_m/\cos\theta_m}e^{-ik_mz_2/\cos\theta_m}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

（a）反射方向。

$E^+$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.5)式和本文(2.29)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m'1}t_{mm'}e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}r_{m2}e^{2i\beta_{m}h_{m}}L^{+}_{m/m}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

$E^-$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.7)式和本文(2.30)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m'1}t_{mm'}e^{i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}}L^{-}_{m/m}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

对于由E⁺源产生的和频光，将z_2从0积分至该层厚度；对于由E⁻源产生的和频光，从该层厚度积分至0。再利用下列关系整理：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{mm',p}L^{+}_{m/m,px}\\ &=L^{-}_{m'/m,px}\\[5pt]t_{mm',s}L^{+}_{m/m,sy}&=L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}\\[5pt]t_{mm',p}L^{+}_{m/m,pz}&=-L^{-}_{m'/m,pz}\\[5pt]t_{mm',p}L^{-}_{m/m,px}&=L^{-}_{m'/m,px}\\[5pt]t_{mm',s}L^{-}_{m/m,sy}&=L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}\\[5pt]t_{mm',p}L^{-}_{m/m,pz}&=L^{-}_{m'/m,pz}
\end{aligned}
$$

$(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m})$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_1^-(0^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m\prime1}e^{i\beta_{m\prime}h_{m\prime}}L^-_{m\prime/m}P_a^*\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\pm r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}\beta_{m,ir}}\pm r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\pm r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\pm r_{m2,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

&=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}t_{m'1,SF,s}e^{i(\beta_{m',SF}+\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}}{1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad \frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+r_{m2,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m,SF}h_{m}}\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\}L^{-}_{m'/m,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}
\end{aligned}\quad(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m})\tag{2.45b}
$$

（b）透射方向。

$E^+$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.6)式和本文(2.29)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m2}e^{i\beta_{m}h_m}(1+r_{1m'}r_{m'm}e^{2i\beta_{m'}h_{m'}})L^{+}_{m/m}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

$E^-$ 源的贡献由文件《一般式》的(2.8)式和本文(2.30)式给出：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}t_{m2}e^{i\beta_{m}h_m}(r_{m'm}+r_{1m'}e^{2i\beta_{m'}h_{m'}})L^{-}_{m/m}P_a^*\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

对于由E⁺源产生的和频光，将z_2从0积分至该层厚度；对于由E⁻源产生的和频光，从该层厚度积分至0。再利用下列关系整理：

$$
\begin{aligned}
&t_{m2,p}L^{+}_{m/m,px}\\ &=L^{+}_{m/2,px}\\[5pt]t_{m2,s}L^{+}_{m/m,sy}&=L^{+}_{m/2,sy}\\[5pt]t_{m2,p}L^{+}_{m/m,pz}&=L^{+}_{m/2,pz}\\[5pt]t_{m2,p}L^{-}_{m/m,px}&=L^{+}_{m/2,px}\\[5pt]t_{m2,s}L^{-}_{m/m,sy}&=L^{+}_{m/2,sy}\\[5pt]t_{m2,p}L^{-}_{m/m,pz}&=-L^{+}_{m/2,pz}
\end{aligned}
$$

$(n_{m\prime\prime}=n_{m})$。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}e^{i\beta_mh_m}L^+_{m/2}P_a^*\frac{i}{\delta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\\&\qquad\mathord{m}(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+1r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\\&\qquad\mathord{m}(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\\&\qquad\mathord{m}(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\\&\qquad\mathord{m}(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\}
\end{aligned}
$$

上符号用于x、y分量，下符号用于z分量。因此，

&\qquad+(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad &\qquad+(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad &\qquad+(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\}L^{+}_{m/2,px}\chi_{x\alpha\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad &\qquad-(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad-(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,p}r_{m'm,SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad-(r_{m'm,SF,p}+r_{1m',SF,p}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\}L^{+}_{m/2,pz}\chi_{z\alpha\beta}

&E_2^+(h_{m\prime}+h_m^+)_{s}\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\&\quad\times e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}+\beta_{m',ir})h_{m'}}e^{i\beta_{m,SF}h_m}(1+r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&1\\&+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}}{1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+(r_{m'm,SF,s}+r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}1\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad (1+r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+(r_{m'm,SF,s}+r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}r_{m2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\bigl[\\[5pt]&\qquad e^{2i\beta_{m\prime,SF}h_{m\prime}}(1+r_{1m',SF,s}r_{m'm,SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(-\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}+\beta_{m,vis}+\beta_{m,ir}}\\ &\qquad+(r_{m'm,SF,s}+r_{1m',SF,s}e^{2i\beta_{m',SF}h_{m'}})\frac{e^{i(\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir})h_{m}}-1}{\beta_{m,SF}-\beta_{m,vis}-\beta_{m,ir}}\bigr]\}L^{+}_{m/2,sy}\chi_{y\alpha\beta}

> **原文疑点（Q107，PDF 32—37，体相积分）：** 作者在此明确指出前面相位处理的欠缺，并说明替换方式；这属于原文推导说明，未反向修改2.1/2.2的式子。原定义P星号内含求和，而随后又写求和，按原样保留；δ记号、端点方向和各积分分母符号不擅自消去。m体相的最终式中全局因子、层标与通式有差异，按原页保留，未作为已确认理论修正。



> **原文疑点（Q111，PDF p33—37，式2.45—2.46）：** 2.45的部分积分分母印为βm,SF+βm,visβm,ir，中间未见加减号，按乘积字形保留；候选为补减号。2.45a的z支前印乘号而非加号，2.46b最后一项的直接贡献多一层m′的SF往返指数，均照录。2.46a/b原页前因子未显式列i/δ，与前一无编号通式不同；不自动补入，候选须结合归一化核验。



> **原文疑点（Q112，PDF p27、p33、p35—36，无编号积分通式）：** p27最后一项的右方括号印在最后一个积分分式之前，此处按原位置保留；候选为移到该分式之后。p33的局部积分分母有vis与ir相乘字形，关联Q111。p35—36无编号通式的成对项之间为可见m字形，照录为m；候选为上下符号∓，不能据末尾分量式静默改写成±。这些通式与随后的分量式仍分别保留原值，均未采用原文修正。

## 3. 三层系（1/m′/m/m″/2）产生的和频光

如开篇所述，为求出这一体系产生的和频电场，需要求张量等比级数之和。因此，以作者当时的能力尚不能完成直至最终表达式的计算。不过，可以求出极化，下面将其列出。

若三个膜层中某一层的厚度可以忽略，则在第2部分所得结果中，(1)把极化表达式替换为下面给出的式子，(2)按下述方式替换与该层外侧界面有关的反射系数和透射系数，即可得到所需表达式。

**当两层m与m′之间夹有厚度可忽略的m″层，形成m/m″/m′系时，作如下替换：**

$$
\begin{aligned}
r_{mm\prime}&\Rightarrow\frac{r_{mm\prime\prime}+r_{m\prime\prime m\prime}}{1+r_{mm\prime\prime}r_{m\prime\prime m\prime}},\qquad t_{mm\prime}\Rightarrow\frac{t_{mm\prime\prime}t_{m\prime\prime m\prime}}{1+r_{mm\prime\prime}r_{m\prime\prime m\prime}}\\[5pt]r_{m\prime m}&\Rightarrow\frac{r_{m\prime m\prime\prime}+r_{m\prime\prime m}}{1+r_{m\prime m\prime\prime}r_{m\prime\prime m}},\qquad t_{m\prime m}\Rightarrow\frac{t_{m\prime m\prime\prime}t_{m\prime\prime m}}{1+r_{m\prime m\prime\prime}r_{m\prime\prime m}}
\end{aligned}
$$

### 3.1 电场振幅的乘积

**1/m′界面的1侧：**

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源（$n=0$ 时）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(0^-)E_{ir,\beta}(0^-)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i2n(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m+h_{m'}^-)E_{ir,\beta}(h_m+h_{m'}^-)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha})(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta})(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF})(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i2n(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.10}
$$

**m/m″界面的m″侧：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m+h_{m'}^+)E_{ir,\beta}(h_m+h_{m'}^+)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[ih_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i(2n+1)(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.11}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_m+h_{m'}^+)E_{ir,\beta}(h_m+h_{m'}^+)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF})(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i2n(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.12}
$$

**m′层中深度z1处：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(z_1)E_{ir,\beta}(z_1)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+\begin{aligned}&r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1+\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+\begin{aligned}&r_{m'm,ir,\beta}(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1+\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i(2n+2)(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.13}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(z_1)E_{ir,\beta}(z_1)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+\begin{aligned}&r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+\begin{aligned}&r_{m'm,ir,\beta}(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i2n(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.14}
$$

**m层中距m′/m界面深度z2处：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+z_2)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+z_2)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\exp[iz_2(k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i(2n+2)(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.15}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+z_2)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+z_2)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(k_{m,vis}\cos\theta_{m,vis}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\exp[iz_2(k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\exp[iz_2(k_{m,ir}\cos\theta_{m,ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m,ir})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i2n(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.16}
$$

**m″层中距m/m″界面深度z3处：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+h_m+z_3)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+h_m+z_3)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',vis}\cos\theta_{m'',vis}(1+\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i(\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}+\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''})}\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',vis}\cos\theta_{m'',vis}(-1+\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',ir}\cos\theta_{m'',ir}(1+\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i(\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}+\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''})}\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',ir}\cos\theta_{m'',ir}(-1+\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',ir})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i(2n+2)(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.17}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&E_{vis,\alpha}(h_{m'}+h_m+z_3)E_{ir,\beta}(h_{m'}+h_m+z_3)\\ &=E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',vis}\cos\theta_{m'',vis}(1-\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i(\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}+\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''})}\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',vis}\cos\theta_{m'',vis}(-1-\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{1\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',ir}\cos\theta_{m'',ir}(1-\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i(\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}+\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''})}\exp[iz_3(k_{m'',ir}\cos\theta_{m'',ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m'',SF}\tan\theta_{m'',ir})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\exp[i2n(h_m\tan\theta_{m,SF}+h_{m\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime,SF}+h_{m\prime\prime}\tan\theta_{m\prime\prime,SF})\\&\qquad\times(-k_{m,ir}\sin\theta_{m,ir}-k_{m,vis}\sin\theta_{m,vis})]
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.18}
$$

### 3.2 和频极化

将上面求得的电场乘积乘以和频过程的二阶非线性极化率，即得到和频极化。再往后的计算极为复杂；作者认为，只有在某一层无限薄时，才可能求得表达式。

**1/m′界面的1侧：**

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源（$n=0$ 时）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(0^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.19}
$$

**1/m′界面的m′侧：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(0^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.20}
$$

**m″/2界面的2侧：**

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_{m'}+h_m+h_{m''}^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}t_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}+\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}t_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}+\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.21}
$$

**m″/2界面的m″侧：**

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_{m'}+h_m+h_{m''}^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha})e^{i(\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}+\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta})e^{i(\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}+\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'})}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.22}
$$

**m′/m界面的m′侧：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_{m'}^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.23}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_{m'}^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}(1+r_{m'm,ir,\beta})[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.24}
$$

**m′/m界面的m侧：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_{m'}^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.25}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_{m'}^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}}[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}}[{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}]}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.26}
$$

**m/m″界面的m侧：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_m+h_{m'}^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha})(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta})(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.27}
$$

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_m+h_{m'}^-)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha})(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta})(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.28}
$$

**m/m″界面的m″侧：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(h_m+h_{m'}^+)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}t_{m'm,vis,\alpha}t_{mm'',vis,\alpha}e^{i(\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,vis}h_{m})}(1+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}t_{m'm,ir,\beta}t_{mm'',ir,\beta}e^{i(\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}+\beta_{m,ir}h_{m})}(1+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}

$E^+$ 源（经反射和透射）与 $E^-$ 源。

\end{aligned}

**m′层中深度z1处：**

$E^+$ 源与 $E^-$ 源（经反射和透射）。

$$
\begin{aligned}
&P_a^*(z_1)\\ &=\sum_{\alpha,\beta}\chi_{a\alpha\beta}E^0_{vis,\alpha}E^0_{ir,\beta}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',vis,\alpha}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}+r_{1m',vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m',vis}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\frac{t_{1m',ir,\beta}}{{\begin{aligned}&\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{1m',ir,\beta}r_{m'm,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&\\&+(r_{m'm,ir,\beta}+r_{1m',ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m',ir}h_{m'}})(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\end{aligned}}}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&+r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+\begin{aligned}&r_{m'm,vis,\alpha}(1+r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})\\&+(r_{mm'',vis,\alpha}+r_{m''2,vis,\alpha}e^{2i\beta_{m'',vis}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,vis}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',vis}\cos\theta_{m',vis}(-1+\tan\theta_{m,SF}\tan\theta_{m',vis})]\}\\[5pt]&\quad\times\{\begin{aligned}&(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&+r_{m'm,ir,\beta}(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(1+\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\\[5pt]&\qquad+\begin{aligned}&r_{m'm,ir,\beta}(1+r_{mm'',ir,\beta}r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})\\&+(r_{mm'',ir,\beta}+r_{m''2,ir,\beta}e^{2i\beta_{m'',ir}h_{m''}})e^{2i\beta_{m,ir}h_{m}}\end{aligned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1+\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\}

ned}\exp[iz_1(k_{m',ir}\cos\theta_{m',ir}(-1-\tan\theta_{m',SF}\tan\theta_{m',ir})]\}

**m层中距m′/m界面深度z2处：**

$$

**m″层中距m/m″界面深度z3处：**

$$

\tag{3.36}
$$



> **原文疑点（Q110，PDF p38—51，三层系公式）：** 3.1及3.19最前端的反射因子含传播指数，原式照录。内部源点正反两类的局部路径相位不同（如3.5/3.6、3.7/3.8），不统一；3.13/3.14与3.31/3.32中m′层vis支角标局部写m,SF，仍保留。体相指数内可见额外左圆括号且未闭合，按原符号保留；m″体相反射项指数还含βmhm项，不删除。以上均为原式记号/分组疑点，候选为按各层传播及配对括号统一，尚未采用。
